KALAM KHMAT 




H a ka jingim jong ngi ki brlew ka don kaet-re-kaei ka 
jingjia ha ki snem ba la leit jong ngi kaba shonshap 
sah ha ki khrnat jong ka jabieng bad ki bor kynmaw 
jong ka. Long kata ha kano kano ka dur: la bha ne sniew, 
la pynkmen ne pynpang nud, phin da leh da kumno 
kuinno ruh ban pynklet ia ka phin ym lah koit. 

Dei naduh ki snem 1945 sha neng ha ki jingpule ia 
ki kot synrop (Rapid Reading) jong ka sobjek Phareng

jong ka matrik la shon shap ha ka Jirigkynmaw jong nga 
ia ki jinghikai ‘Kama! ia ka Hok T (Moral values) na ka 
Olib Garden ia kaba nga ring dur mynta sha ka ‘Brisoh 
Olib bad ai ha phi ki nongpule. 

Nga tyrwa ia ka ha ki long-ling long-sem bad ha ki 
kliun samla Khynroo Khyllud u Hynniewtrep ban kbiat 
ban kjit na ki Ar Jingieid : ieid Ri bad ieidsamla bai'aineh 
hakhap ka syier ka ma da ka Hok katkum ka Hukum 
kaba pasiaw hapoh dohnud jong man uwei pa uwei na 
ngi. La pynshet nongrim ia kane ka parom ‘thawhi’ ha ki 
jingjia histori ba shisha Jong ka thma bah pyrthei ha 
kaba tang da shitroh u pyrton ba bat hukum bad ngeit 
sneng jong ka Kris u la lah ban pynher krat ia u shipai u 
swar ba shu kem kuli da ki hajar hajar da ki nong Per- 
sia. Da kane ka jingjop la pyllait ia ka pyrthei na kaba 
shah pynduhjaid ia ka ‘Synshar Paidbah’ ha sla pyrthei. 

Nga ia ong Hynniewtrep : te kata nga la pynrung 
bun ki ktien bad ki jingong na ki dialek jong kine ki lum 
jong ngi namarba nga kwah ha ngin pynroi ia ka ktien 


5 


jong ngi da kaba shim bad (adapt) pyniadei na kiwei ki 
jingkren Jong u paid hynniewtrep na SHATEI - SHATHIE 
= MIHNGI = SEPNGI. ban ia kaba shim Than na phareng 
bad Dkhar. 

Naduh mynsiem nga sngewnguh ia ka Department of 
Higher Education kaba la kyrshan ia nga ban pynmih ia 
kane ka kot. Da kaba kyrpang nga ainguh ia Ma A. 
Rymbai, Inspector of School E.K. uba la leh shitom ban

pyniyllih (pynjem) ia ka lynti sha Director of Public In- 
struction. 

Ba ar, ngam lah khlem da sngewnguh ia u Rev. J. 
Cilia sdb uba la shah jynsur mynsiem ia nga ban 
pyndonkam ia ka type writer ha Guwahati ha ka por ba 
ka thij ka pyrkhat jong nga ka shong syier ha tmit ka 
lyhuh. Khublei shibun eh ia phi. Nga dei ban sngewnguh 
ruh Ia i bah H.L. Pde ba la pule bniah ia ka manuskrip 
bad ai ki jingmut ba pynbha la ka ktien jong ngi. 

Nga ai khublei la ka Press DB. bad u ia Bro. Valeri na 
ka bynta ki jinglehshitom ban pynitynnad i'a ka kot. Nga 
ai nguh ruh la kito kiba la iarap i’a nga da kumne kumtai 
Nga kynmaw ruh la i bah J.S. Shangplaing iba la pule i’a 
ka manuskrip jong nga la bun snem bynta bad iba la 
kwah ba kane ka kot kan mih pyrthei na ka bynta ban 
pynroi la ka thoh ka tar Khasi jong ngi.

KA BRISOH OLIB

L ada ngam bakla, kane ka jingjia ka la jia ha ki spah 
snem 522-433 B.C. ha ka juk u nongthoh Greek ba 
pawnam, u Pindar uba la im naduh ka snem 518-432 
BC. Ka histori ruh ka kren shaphang ka jingwan hlar 
thma u Xerxes ka Persia, hynrei uba la shah rem ha 
Salamis u snem 480BC. 

Klto kiba ju pule, pelt ha ki phlim ia kl 
jingiathuhkhana, ka Helen jong ka Troy, ki Spartan, kl 
lah ban mut-dur ia kl jingjia ba don ha kane ka sain- 
khana. U tyngshop ba kham paw eh del u Kleander bad 
kiwei pat ki tyngshop ki puron kiba naduh ba ki dang 
khynnah la pynkilan i'a ki ba kin pynlong doh pynlong 
snam ia ka jingieid ri haduh ba ki la lah ban beh krad i'a 
u nongwan hiar thma, u Xerxes u khun u Darius I. U 
Kleander ki para bad kumjuh u mraw ki la im ka jingim 
bakhuid ba suba haduh kaba kut ki da ioh ka burom ka 
hormot ha ka shongkha shongman. 

Kiba nga bang eh ki long ki ktien kynnoh (imitatives 
or akin words) bad ki kyntien na ri Pnar. Tang mar ia 
plied phin lap ki kyntien ‘khlik-khlik khlak-khlak, ka 
khru-khru ru-ru, ka bshem basakar bakylluid rwang. 
Nangta, Jingshla basngewkiat ka pynkriang ia ka Iyer*; ‘U 
Pheidippedes, jkeng sawsniehdoh* bad bun kiwei kiwei ki 
kyntien ba la ai rong ai rup da u nongpynkylla, u Rangbah 
Michael D. Kahit, Nga kynmaw i’a ka Jingruwai, The brook* 
kaba lada pule ha ka jingjar jar, ngin iohsngew i’a ka sur 
kato hi ka wahduid. Kumjuh ruh ia kane ka kot KA 
BRISOH OLIB, lada ngi pule ha ka jingjaijar, ngin lap ia

7 


ka jingshisha ba une u Rangbah Michael D. Kahit, u long 
uwei pat na ki nongthoh Kliasi uba tbit bha ha ka airong 
ai rup ia ki ktien (A word painter) Shuh shuh la buh la 
ktien Khasi ba shisien pule ngin kum pyrkhat ba kito 
briew ha kane ka khana ki kren da ka Khasi. 

Kyrmen ba u Rangbah Michael D. Kahit un nang sei 
shuh shuh na ka thiar jong ka khuin pyrkhat ba la ih 
bha jong u sa kiwei pat ki buit sha ka thoh ka pule ban 
kiew rasong ka roi ka par ka ktien Khasi. 


L. H. Pde

E5T 



I 

HAPOH IEW 



Ka iew ha A then,

N alor ka klik-klik klak-klak na ka langLhari, ka 
khru-khru, ru-ru sharum sha nerig. ka kiak kiak 
kak-kak ki shyoh (ryndang) kynthei ym don eiei hapoh 
ka ling ba shong kyndup jlang marwei ha kyndong, 
ka snrok Apollo. Kum kiwei pat ki iing ha Athen, ka 
jong u Arkhelans. u basan ka shnong, ka long ka 
i'ingsawdong ba la kynroh muri ia ka phyllaw, ne 
bshem, ba sakar bakylluid rwang. Ha u rum u neng 
ka paw tang ka kynroh sada ba la pynpei tang da ka 
jynkhangbah, hynrei hapoh, ki tyngkong ne kamra 
baplie sha ka dahari (beranda) ba la ker, ki shai bad 
ki phalang, ki i-syaid bad i-juh, ki i-shngaifl de ba la 


9 



tawah da kl pyrda suitrong bad slang da kl syngkhien 
bamom-mom. Hapdeng ka bshem don ka duwan 
‘leilongi'ing. Haphang shathie don ka jaka shong ba 
da u maw marbyl bad bun kl mula, ki lyngknot ba la 
pynbeit shriak hapoh syrnglew batngen Jong ki tnad 
bapyiar na u dieng sohjew; sawdong, kl syntlew 
lynguin ha kl bun khap kynroh, ki diengrit ohshrong 
bad tiewlyngksiar ki plied shylluid phlar-l’phiar ha kl 
khiew ranei tah rong. 

Thik marpyrshah ka khyrdop ba plie sha surok 
na daharl u Kleander, u khun jong u Arkhelaus, 
l’ngang u shaniah ha u pa'ia. U lah ban i'ohsngew i'a 
ka sur kyrkieh-awri-awra jong i mei lajong, jong ki 
kynthei bad jong ki mraw hapoh ka ling thaiftjain. 

Teng teng ki para jong u, ka Iunis bad ka Klaris,

ling ling ki march synrud i'a u, ki i'arah i'a ki song 
jain-ni ne ki jyndeng kordor ba ki mraw ki dang 
i'athep ha ki kriah, klaw, da thlien lynga. u peit ia ki. 
U dang khadar snem ka ryta, lymphoift ka main ka 
meifl ba i-ngab, u shniuh kyllaih i'akli u pynwan ia 
ka dur sha ki khmat pylliaw bajngum suinbneng 
kiba klum i'a baroh kat kaba i'ohi ha ka shisien peit. 
Ka jain tawah babyrngum jong u ka long da ki saithir 
nylla, la pyndait da ki pin 'nonglieh na sha tyrpeng 
bad ka noh pynwan dur jwain i'a ki ksangkti bad kjat 
baksah, sawsyang ba thang ka sngi. 

Ka rengmat ka ran. Dynghang u peit lyngngoh 
i'a kane ka khrum-khrum khram-khram, ka lynga, 
ka lyngngit. Kaei kane ka khih lyngkhum! Ka mang 
aiu kane ka song ka teh! Balei kane ka kren klak- 


10 



klak kum bym lapllengl Imat ym don ba laittl laitjat 
ban pyrwa i'a u. U mraw ba del lalam skul i'a u ruh 
um don, u la lett bad u Arkhelaus shabar te mynta 
ka sngl ym don jingkhreh kot satia. U iw kaei-re-kael 
kaba pher ka ban jla. Ka mariang hi ka ktik pynskuifi 
i'a u. Kumno i'a u khun i mei nba la khadar snem yn 
set lang hangne bad ki kynthei haba ik-bad-ir ha 
surok ha khiin kl hamsaia ki pyndap i'a ka Iyer da ka 
jingsawa ba kynsha. 

Jrut u kynthuit la ki tyrpeng, bad syik u syngit 
la u saipan, u phai ka met sha ka syrngiew batngen 
jong ka phyllaw bad bloit u mih sha surok. Ym don

briew! 

Ka sngi Lyiur ka thang pynshrang ha ka 
jingthaba khrek- khrek i'a ki i'ing balieh jong ka Athen 
bad, ha shyngkup kynroh, ki syrngiew ki noh ngaw- 
Ingaw kum u fiiut diengiong. Na sha ki phang ka i'ew 
wan sawa ka huri-hura jong u paidkar, ka shu kie 
bad lngaid sawa hum-hum ba ker ki kynroh. 

Klir-klir shat ki khmat u Kleander. Kane te ka 
pran bashlan shisha! Ban shu lait-tuh ei shapoh sor 
khlem uto u Ksanthias ba-i-ngiah bad bud dien 
kynthep kynthep man ka sngi ba leit skul. Tang 
shen u sngewshngairi i'a ka lynti bad u i'aid biang 
biang, u klar na ka ktieh bad dien kali, u ryngkang 
i'a ka synrum synreh bathud tengkhat hakhmat jong 
u. Katba nangjfaid, nangjam ka sur bad, khadduh, 
haba ka i'ew ka plied hakhmat jong u, la i kumba ka 
Luri Lura ka thnam kynshu biang i'a ka iwtung u 
ksew. 


11 



Ki khmat phoilia jong u khynnah ki peit khling- 
khlang. Hapoh syrngiew ki paia ba Seng shriak 
sawdong ka shyngkup Sew don kyntoit kyntoit ki 
kynhun briew, ki khmat jong ki laift-laifi ki sawkyrang 
da ka jingbitar, don patde ki batyrkhong raw raw da 
ka pisa-ka-lynga. Kiwei patde shibit shane shibit 
shatai ki rah khubor, ki wad jingtip; ha ki khmat la 
thoh phalang Sa ka klang pangnud bahar-harrukom, 
ka khih ka ksar ba phieng ba-i-ngiew.

Na sha ka dong jong ki basa bad ki shlem jong 
ki kamar ki riew ting-shi-ting ki tyrnem halor ki 
rynniang, bsut-shi-bsut pyrhut ki kynwo; ting-ting 
shane, khar khar dar-dar shatai riew ki tupia nar, ki 
jymphong shadem, ki waitlam, ki jynsop’ti jynsop’jat: 
ki shipai arsut artet ki kyrdem bad pynkyrkieh Sa ki 
kamar. Ki nongdie basa hapdeng Sew ki i bymniewkor 
la ka die ka thied. Don kiba la iehnoh la ki basa, ban 
i'alum sha u paidbah baialang lyinbung khlem da 
pyrwa Sa ki ksew makia ne khynnah rit ba shang 
syntiat ban ap kabu. Don ruh kiba lum bad song 
kyrkieh ne tliep kulmar la ki mar ha shang bad 
khreh ban khang Sew noh. 

Sa ba u Kleander un Sawot (shimrtiat) Sa u soh 
apel ba-ih hoin hoifi na kawei ka basa ha syndah 
jong u te lap ba patied ki kyrdem kjat ba Said kadom 
shipai ka da tyllep artad Sa ka kaw-kaw daw-daw 
Jong u paidbhur Sew. Na shaphang ka Akropolis shi- 
kynhun shikynhun u pyrton phong-sopnar bad la ka 
stieh ka wait ha kti u Said i'atur jram kynjram lyngba 
ka madan Sew. Ka sngi ka phalang khlek khlek Sa ki 
wait ki sum jong ki. U paidbhur u pyrkhun shadiang 
bad shamon ban plaid lynti Sa u pyrton hapdeng bad 


12 



sa khyrdep blang lien na sha dien kum ka um jong 
ka durlaw Ejian bajngum kaba tap ia ka dar jong ka 
lieng Laikymah-boitha (laisyrtap) kaba lur ha sla Jong 
ka. Ha ryngkhi jong u pyrton laid pyneh khohwah u

khynnah, da kaba kyniw, u khawoit sha la u para 
khynnah skul. u Demetrius. 

“Ap, Demetrius, ap” u pyrta ia mareh sha la u 
paralok. 

“Ngam lah ap me” pyrta kylla u Demetrius da 
kaba, sngewshrai. “Nga dang bud i’a kine ki shipai te 
me!” 


“Shaei te ki ialeit?” kylli u Kleander, katba dang 
iamareh marshruh. “Bad ka jia aiu? Balei u paidbah 
kumne thlim?” 

“Shuh me, wat pynthlia Ta nga," kynhied u 
Demetrius sa ka lehsa de. “Me dang khunlung ban 
sngewthuh.” 

Ka snam u Kleander ka kynroi da ka jingbitar. 
Hato ymdei tang ar snem ba u Demetrius u shwa ia 
u hynrei, pynban u kham proh bad kham stad ma u 
ha skul? Rdin u snoh na ka jain tawah u paralok 
haduh ba lait sa u pin ba pyndait ha tyrpeng bad u 
Demetrius um banse ban sangeh ban tarn ia u. 

“Shuh ho lih, wat pynsalia ia nga," u kynhiet 
lem bad ka kawut de ia u Kleander, hynrei, u patde 
ngoit u dem bad u lait artad nangta u sah de sbak, 
ki ia pyneh artad. 

Ha-mar iamut shisha hi te lap ba shah bat noh 
ha tyrpeng tyrpeng bad da ka sur jai bad jem, “Balei, 
balei kumne?”

13 



Dang ing hi laift-lairi u Kleander u kren phak 
phak la kaba u la kwah, “Nga kwah ban tip kaei 
kane ka jingkhynwin,” u batai, “bad balei u paid 
baroh u lakren hynflium-hyniiiam kumne, bad shael 
kl ialeit kine ki shipai, u Demetrius pat um treh 
i'athuh." “U dang rit eh ban sngewthuh,” leh bakren 
heh u Demetrius. 

U briew baieng hapdeng Jong ki u phong ka 
tupia nar bad ka jynda shadem jong ki shipai riam- 
nar hynrei um pat khlem rah la ka stieh. Ka khmut 
ka khmat te rngaift hynrei ka ishongkhia, u ftiuhmoh 
bakdor samup bad ki khmat kbeit shongshan. Haba 
u phuh sammut patde, ka main bakhor domriang ka 
Jem lhin bad ki khynnah ki sngewbynriew noh. 

“Ale shane sha kyndong”, u khot, “bad ngan 
i'athuh ia phi i'a kaba la jia.” 

Da kaba kmen ki khynnah ki bud ia u sha ka 
syrngiew jong uwei u pa'ia, ka jingbitar ruh ka la jah 
noh kumba ka la wan ryngkhat hi. 

“Phi la ju i'ohsngew shaphang ki Persia?" thaw 
kylli uta u-riewpher. 

“ Hooid keifl,” kloilan jubab u Kleander, “ki shong 
shatai sha shiliang ka durlaw bad ki don u syiem 
bapawnam ba synshar ia ki”.

“Kaba del.” U kem ktien phuhmat;” Hynrei nalor 
kata don sa kiwei de kiba ong shaphang jong ki. Uta 
u syiem bakhraw uba synshar ia ki u del u syiem 
iuhtop amrang. Um ai jinglaitluid ia la ki raiot, bad 
un jin da knieh ne tuh ruh la ka jinglaitluid jong 
kiwei pat ki briew. Napdeng kito ia kiba u la jop ki 


14 



don ruh ki jald ki kynja jong ngi sha shlliang um ha 
ki nongbah jong ka Ionia.” 

“Kumta keift," knieh ktien u Kleander," nga tip 
ba ki la i'akiat pyrshah la u Darius hynrei u la 
fiiombor bad u la pyndem la ki.” 

“Kaba del, thik,” jubab u nongwei, naduh kata 
ka por u Darius jong ka Persia u la sni bad mang 
ban thombor pyrshah ki nong Athen kum ka kput ba 
ki la ai jingj'arap ia la ki jaid ki kynja. Man ka sngi, 
hana ki ong, haba ki mraw ki i'eng hakhmat jong u 
ki kren pynrhah: “Syiem kynrad. u dusmon u dandia 
u Athenl" Te kumta u syuh ingtyrkhong lnger-shi- 
lnger (hala ki por) i'a ngi. Mynnymwei u la wan king 
shi lyhien ki lieng pyrshah ia ngi hynrei ki blei ki la 
don bad ngi. Ka eri'ong ka la kynting khie dew te 
baroh kata ka lyhien (kynhun) liengi'apom ka la shah 
kynting sapra-saphret bad jakhom ei. Mynta u 
pyrshang biang. Ha kane ka pali te ka lyhien ka la 
lur khlem jingeh shaduh Kris bad ki shipai Persia ki 
la i'uh kjat ha Marathon.”

“Me sngew lih, kata'?” la kren heh u Demetrius. 
“Me tip mynta balei u paid u kulmar thlim bad balei 
ki shipai ki ia pynstet sha rud nong Athen. Hynrei 
un i'alah shaei pyrshah ki nong Persia?” 

Uba khamrit u shu peit mrong sha u riewphylla. 

I pa i i'athuh ba ki nong-Sparta ki long ki shipai 
bashlurtam ha pyrthei,” u kren roh shongshit. “Kim 
wan mo ban iarap Ia ngi?” 

U nongwei u jyllud la u ftiuhtmaifi shiphang 
kum ban khan ban khad shwa ban jubab. 


15 



“Ki don la kl jong ki korbar-sorbar,” u kren 
khadduh eh, hynrei kynmaw la kane khynnah: Ka 
jinglaltluid ka dei ka jingal ba kor duh ba ai kl blei, 
hynrei ym al i'a ka khlem kirmit ne jingiakut. Kl 
briew kl del ban pynlong i'alade kl babiang bablt ban 
'ioh la ka. Kynmaw sa kane putet: haba kawei ka 
nongbah ka duhnoh la ka jlnglailluid la teh syngit 
ruh i’a ka jinglaitluid jong u paid kyllum. la kane ka 
mat ki Sparta kim pat i'a pel satia." 

Kl ktien kl ring da ka sur jylliew, bad da kaba 
khmied u Kleander u i'ohi liak i'a ki khmat ba shai 
klir-klir bad ka maifi-ka-mein jong u nongwei ka 
tyrha rhem. 

“Uba i'aleh na ka bynta ka Athen, u laleh na ka

bynta ka Kris bad ka mynsiem ka sngar jong ka 
Helas,” u kren shit mynsiem, “Un pynsah ka kyrteng, 
ka nam ha la ka jaid ban pyrto u para-ri”. 

Mar hangta u samla ba la riam-dap shipai u 
laid synlad then-mthen. U bat i'a ka ksangkti u 
paralok kl khynnah bad ring noh i'a u na ki. 

“Ale noh, Eskhilus,” u khot kyrkieh, “don ki 
kam bah sha khmat. U Peidiphides u la wan phai na 
Sparta bad u leit al kaiphod sha kl jeneral. Phi pat 
hangne phi Ta byrngia parom bad arngut kl laitlan." 

“Ki dei ban tip, Kinekeiros, kl dei ban 
sngewthuh," la phai ktien u riewpher da ka bajem 
jai, “Kl del ban i'eng bad ap mriang i'a ka Jinglaltluid 
ynda maphl bad manga ngl la lyngngaid noh sha 
lyndet." 


16 



Da kaba phuhsamrkhie bad khublei u phai, u 
ieh noh la ki khynnah ba i'ai'eng hajan pala ban pelt 
shairi na shalyndet ba u laid. U Kleander u bat khat 
i'a ka ksangtl u paralok. 

“Kloi, Demetrius, - shit-lr u paslaw," la ngin la 
bud ban pelt kan jia al'u, ngln lah, tyndi-tynda, ban 
Yohsngew eiel ." 

Sla bad sla ki khynnah ki bud dien, ki plaid na 
khap paidbah ki khyngngoid bad khun na lwiar ki

paia, mynta pat, bloit sha ki basa iew bad ki shlem 
kamar sha basawang ka Yew, sak bad sak ki thew 
sha ka phang i'ingdorbar kaba don ha syntai u lum 
ha phud Yew. Ki paYa ki ker sawdong ia ka tyngkong 
dorbar bad ki Yai'eng hangta ha syrngiew jong uwei 
na ki, 

“Hel, la wan ki jeneral," paslaw u Demetrius. 
“Pelt, utai u Melsaides. Iiw, uba la leh hangamei noh 
tehl Uba i'a ong suda ba u la ju long u ’riew lehheh 
banbeift ha ki Juk jong u." 

“Uei utai, Demetrius?’’ kylli u Kleander. “utai u 
baYong shniuh klaw klaw bad ki khmat klir klir? Peit 
kumno u kren 'iarkhie artad. Khlempep utai uwei pat 
te u del u lok Janpran jong u mo. Peit, peit, u tawah 
kti ha tyrpeng jong u.” 

U Demetrius u rkhie kynphuit batip eh. “U lok? 
maitang, paralok keifll Khambun ka i'ashut Ya-aireng. 
Hynrei u Themistokles u tip kumno ban phur sabuit- 
sayian i'a la ki nongshun ruh ban pynmyllen i'a u. I 
pa i shait i'a han kyntlen phawer ba u Themistokles 
un leh ki kam bah lawei la ar ha Athen. U tei u

2

Ka shariot hyndat

ka kti, kiba ktud waid-lwaid sha surok Athen

HALOR KA HERKULES 


K a jingklng lieng ka la long kaba suk na ka 
ryndang-um sha Artemisium. Bad, hadlen

jingpadiah phawar ba pynbud ia ka jingknia step.

ka jinglehheh, lehmeng, u pyni ba u kheifi dewthala

sa ia une u lamwir? Ka hok ai'u u don ban kren?

ma-u u bym don shnong ban kam la kajong un 
don ktien ha kane ka jingrai? Ade, ban i'eng khlieh 
briew ruh um dei ban 'ioh u nong klang ba khlem 
Hng, khlem shnong, u iaidwir u iaidrhab tang kata hi”. 

U shu shong lyngngeit bad bun na ki para 
kapten ki shu sngewphrieh 'iamrem ba ki sngewthuh- 
sngewmat ia ka jingpoh bad jtngkynshleifi jong kane

ka jingkynthah pharep ia u rangbah jong palat

sngi, bad hangta ngin tei ka nongbah bad ka raij 


184 



i'alade, bad phi lah ban Jyllop ne Jngi katba phi 
mon khlem ma ngi." 

Kane ka jingkren kaba da mut kam shisha da 
uta u nongialam bashlur ka la biang hi ban pynnoh 
Ta ka tarajur. Khlem da ainia shuh dano dano ruh, 
putet, khlem da khalai, u luribides u rai. 

“Ngin i’a aireng hangne ha Salamis,” u hukum. 
“Duhai, pynksan ki blei ia ka daw jong ngi ban 
dule ki nong Athen.” 

Ka jingialang ka la wai. Ki kapten ki ialeit sha 
la ki jong ki jong ki lleng ban pynkhreh la ka nusib 
kaba ka sngi bathymmai kan wan rah.

XV 

KA THMA HA SALAMIS 



Kserses u peitrong ia ka thma Salamis. 


S hllyngnger (baroh klta ki por) ha ka sngi babud, 
ka saham jingartatien bad jingsngewiftdang, 
(sngewlynga-pisa) ka sdien halor ka lhienlieng Jong 
ki Krik. Ym don dak ban latur na ki Persia, bad ki 
Krik kim kwah ban pyndik satia. Baroh shisngi ki 
lieng kynda ki ia king kyllaifl na pdeng ki liengheh 
katba ki kapten bad ki ophisar ki iatalad kam. Ynda 
la khamnoh ka sngi u Themistokles u kiew sha ka 
’Yiem KhyUiuL Ki jingkhuslai bad jingduhthiah ki la 
pynsawtriej la ki khmat Jong u lait na kane u long 
kumjuh hi- Jylluifl, jwaifl, skhem ka Jingmut, stot 
ka jlngngelt, ba paral bashynroiflriap . U la ju niewkor 
shlbun ia u Ameinias bad u Kleander te u leit sha ki 
ban pelt ki pyrkhat kumno ia kane ka shyngkhan.

pynhiar nlak la ka stleh, te ha kata ka salit por u

Du wan, ki blei longiing, ki mawbynna, 

Mawshyieng, mawniam, Mawbah, kulong ki Kthaw. 
La It-laka na kti Persia b'nym mraw”. 

Hu-u-lahu, Kserses 

Ka jingkynku hung heng khru ru jong u 
Jubab ngi ai. Ka ktien kam lap kynshu 
La. da mar lieng mar lieng ki khmut barnong 
La dung tynrah. Ki Krik tynsah nyngkong. 

Sidonia la phot, la khrai shyrtong 

fa bud kiwei, mar shruh, mar khmat, kyrtong! 


211

Ki Persia jlang thiaw.. 

Pynkiang u latfl nyngkong, ki eh ki shan, 
Hynrei kumno hangta ha khim ba syam 
Ban phai ki lah katta shrem rem ki bun! 

Na lieng i'a lieng ym long Yarap pynkhun. 
Sangsot! Da khmut ar prong ki puh lajong, 
Boitha ki dait, ki kdlah, ki ot lyphong. 

Ade! Ki Krik, da thew, da mut, da mang 
Ki ker paron, ki niom bad pom naphang. 

Aa, ngil 

Ka shram, ka biar lybuit-buij-buij khlem shnet 
Kylla (shang) prah kyndui-ly-dui ka met 
Haduh ka um kam paw ba klup byrthap. 

Bad khnong, bad lyphur bad met baper la Yap 
La tap lin lin ia um, ia maw da SmerTyrut. 

Ki lieng jong ngi ki kdat lynsher 
Phet krat umbah.,.. 

,...Ka Yam lynniar ka per kylleng sawdong 
Naduh ryndang shaduh iyngkew la rong 

Jong ki bamong ia ki bai’ap 

Haduh ka miet da jain matlah ka tap. 
(Aeskhilus: Ki nong Persia)

La pra la ngam jyllop 
212 



SHI KYNTIEN IA-ONG 

K a jingroi ka ktien lyngba ka thoh ka pule ka pynphalang 7a ka jingsan 
(a KTIEN. Ka jingong ba ka Ktien Khasi ka duk, ka mih na kito kibym 
kloi ban pdiang ia ka shimkylliang kyntien, la napoh la ka Ri na ki 'tien khun 
(dialect) ne wat na shabar ki kynroh jong ka. Ka ktien English la ong ba ka 
long ka ktien kaba riewspah tam ha ka pyrthei ka thoh ka pule; ka daw bah 
jong kane ka long tang na ka daw ba kane ka Ktien ka iar mynsiem ban 
pdiang kyntien na kiwei kiwei de ki jait ktien. 

Ha kane ka kot Ka Brisoh Olib, ba la ring ksai sha ka ktien u 
Hynniewtrep ne Ktien Khasi da u Rangbah Michael D. Kahit na Barato jong 
ka dong ka Ri Khasi, ka Ri Pnar, ngin shem ba u nongthoh um shym la 
tyngkan ban shim kylliang kyntien Khasi la na Ri Pnar ne wat na ki kyntien 
nongwei haba u shem ba kum ia kine ki kyntien ym shem tynrai na la ka 
jong ka thymmei. Kiba kum kine ki kyntien i'apan kylliang ne ki kyntien na ki 
Ktien khun ka Ri ki don byllai ha kane ka kot kiba halai-halai kin sa kylla 
kyntien Khasi hi kumba long kiwei kiwei ki kyntien iapan na ka Ktien Sanskrit, 
Hindi ne ktien Phareng. Da kumne ngi la nang lapynroi ia ka thiar ki kyntien 
jong ka Ktien Khasi. 

Kawei kaba ngi dei ban iaroh ia une u nongthoh ka long ban iohi ba u 
da lit bmah ia ki kyntien ba u pyndonkam. U la lum ia ki kyntien kiba la ju 
lapyndonkam ha ki por ba la leit noh ia kiba ngi la leh klet ban iapyndonkam 
mynta. Dei da kumne ba u la lum la lang ia kine ki kyntien kiba dang sah khyrdong 
hangne hangtai ba ioh ki jah noh na ka rukom iapyndonkam paitbah mynta.

Ka Ktien Khasi, la ong wat da ki nongwei hi ruh ba ka long ka Ktien 
kaba riewspah eh ha ka liang ki ‘Adverb’ ne ki ‘Intensifier’ kiba da ka 
jingpyndonkam ia ki ngi lah ban pynduna ia ka jingbun kyntien ha ka kren 
ka khana ne ka thoh ka pule. Kane ka kot kan sakhi ia kane ka jingong. 

Sa shi ban, ka jingpule 7a ki Translation’ ka la rung kum kawei na ki 
Course ia kiba la pule ha University. Da ka jingpynwan Khasi ia kane ka 
novel Olib Garden jong u J.H. Macklehose, la nang pynkha roi ia kane ka 
phang jong ka pule puthi. Ngi dang donkam shibun ki ‘translation’ la ki dei 
na ka Ktien nongwei sha ka Ktien Khasi ne na ka Ktien Khasi sha kiwei pat 
ki jait ktien bariewspah jong ka pyrthei, kane ka kot kan ai mynsiem ia kiwei 
ki para nongthoh ban iapyniap sngi de ha kum kane ka phang.
